# PhyloPic icons: attribution

**Source:** [PhyloPic](https://www.phylopic.org), a free database of organism silhouettes created by T. Michael Keesey,
with silhouettes uploaded by many contributors. The images were read through PhyloPic's official public API
(`api.phylopic.org`, build 562, 2026-11-14) and their vector files from `images.phylopic.org` (`vector.svg`: PhyloPic's
own vector version of each upload, pure black). Every icon keeps PhyloPic's attribution for it, verbatim (the Creator
column below), and links its image page on PhyloPic (the Page column).

**License:** per icon, the one its uploader chose, as the image's record on PhyloPic states it: Public Domain Mark 1.0
or CC0 1.0 (no credit required), CC BY 3.0 or CC BY 4.0 (credit required). See `LICENSE.txt` and the License column
below. In this pack: 2,220 icons under CC0, 283 marked public domain, 456 under CC BY 4.0 and 219 under CC BY 3.0.
Images under NonCommercial or ShareAlike licenses are not in this pack.

**How PhyloPic asks to be credited** ("Usage of Images", https://www.phylopic.org/articles/image-usage, as it was on
2026-11-14):

> Images under a Creative Commons Attribution Deed (CC-BY 3.0 or CC-BY 4.0) must be credited to the creator(s). The
> attribution text can be found on Image Pages. You must also provide a link to the license. And, if you have made any
> significant changes to the images, you must indicate what they are.

and: "Attributing images to PhyloPic is never required. (But mentioning the site is always appreciated!)" Public
domain and CC0 images: "You may use them anywhere, in any way, without attribution."

So an icon under CC BY 3.0 or CC BY 4.0 is credited with its taxon, its creator as PhyloPic gives it and its license
(with the license's link), and the credit says that the icon was changed, for example: *Icons: Colinus virginianus by Edwin Price, phylopic.org, CC BY 4.0, adapted.* SiliCoDraw
writes such a line on the page when one of these icons is used. With more than three of them on a page the line names
only PhyloPic; Page → Copy full icon credits then gives the complete list (taxon, creator, page, license with its link)
for the figure legend or the acknowledgments. PhyloPic states no year for an image, so the credits carry none.

**What SiliCoDraw changed:** each `vector.svg` was converted to SiliCoDraw's icon format with `dev/icon-import`: fitted
into a 100 × 100 box, metadata removed, coordinates rounded, and the color made recolorable in the editor. Each icon was
drawn and compared with the original on a white and on a dark page. Each icon is named by its taxon as PhyloPic names it ("Mus
musculus"), which the credits print; the common names PhyloPic lists for the taxon, in many languages, are search words
("house mouse" finds it).

**What was chosen, and what was left out** (13,354 images on PhyloPic, 11,385 of them under public domain, CC0 or
CC BY; 3,178 in this pack):

- images under NonCommercial or ShareAlike licenses (1,969): SiliCoDraw's users publish, and PhyloPic itself warns that
  NonCommercial "includes most publications";
- human figures (63: the clade *Homo* and the other hominin genera);
- public-domain or CC0 images whose attribution says that they were traced from someone else's photograph or drawing
  (122), unless that original is in the public domain by its age (an illustration published before 1926, or by an
  illustrator who died long ago: Joseph Smit, Ernst Haeckel, John Gould, Charles R. Knight and others) or as a work of
  the U.S. federal government (NOAA, the National Park Service, the Fish and Wildlife Service): whoever traces another
  person's work cannot give up that person's rights. Under CC BY such an image is kept, since PhyloPic's attribution
  names the original creator and the credit names everyone it names;
- a selection, so that the pack stays a few thousand icons spread over the tree of life: one image per taxon
  (public domain and CC0 first, then the taxon's primary image on PhyloPic, then the oldest upload; 2,589 other
  images of a taxon left out), one taxon per genus (model organisms, then taxa with a common name, first; a species written with its subgenus, "Nuculana (Saccella) confusa", counts for its genus; 2,064),
  and at most a few hundred per group (birds, mammals, fishes, insects, plants and the others; preferred and commonly
  named taxa first, the rest spread evenly over the genera; 3,211);
- two images that show only part of a head, cut off at the edges (`exclude` in the pack's description), found when every
  icon was looked at on contact sheets;
- 156 images that did not convert (151 over 40 KB after conversion: tracings of fine hair, setae or branching; 3 too long and thin for an icon; 2 holding an embedded picture).

There are no logos or trademarks among PhyloPic's images.

SiliCoDraw is not affiliated with or endorsed by PhyloPic or by the creators of its silhouettes.
